#### [4. Awesome Amazon Seller](/content/ScaleLeap/awesome-amazon-seller/README.md)

##### Software and Tools

* [BulkBarcode Generator](https://bulkbarcode-generator.com) - Makes FNSKU, UPC, EAN-13, Code 128 and QR labels in bulk from an Excel or CSV file, and exports print-ready PDF rolls for thermal printers. Runs in the browser. Free plan.
* [CorpusIQ](https://www.corpusiq.io) - Connects ChatGPT, Claude and Perplexity read-only to Amazon Seller, Shopify, GA4, QuickBooks and 40+ other systems, and answers questions about the business with the source of each number. From $29.95 a month, with a 30-day free trial.
* [Fbaleaks](https://fbaleaks.com) - Audits FBA inventory and reimbursement reports in the browser. Finds lost or damaged units that Amazon did not reimburse, sorts SKUs to keep, reprice or remove, and drafts appeal letters. Free tools and a one-time paid audit.
* [PixVibe](https://pixvibe.ai/tools/remove-background) - Browser-based background remover that exports product photos on a white or transparent background, with tools to compress, resize and crop images. Free for single images, with no account.
* [TranslateMyImage](https://translatemyimage.com/amazon-image-translator) - Translates the text inside product images, infographics, A+ modules and size charts into other languages, and keeps the original layout. Works from the final JPG or PNG files. Three free test images.

##### Product Research and Pre-Launch

* [Trends MCP](https://www.trendsmcp.ai) - MCP server and REST API with trend data from Amazon, Google Search, Google Shopping, TikTok, YouTube and other sources, for demand research in an AI assistant. Free plan with 100 requests a month. [GitHub (⭐44)](https://github.com/trendsmcp-ai/Trends-MCP) ⭐ 44 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-08-29

##### Multiple languages / [Other](#other-1)

* [CodeQue (⭐158)](https://github.com/codeque-co/codeque) ⭐ 158 | 🐛 20 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-05-05 — Ecosystem for structural matching JavaScript and TypeScript code. Offers search tool that understands code structure. Available as CLI tool and Visual Studio Code extension. It helps to search code faster and more accurately making you workflow more effective. Soon it will offer ESLint plugin to create your own rules in minutes to help with assuring codebase quality.
* [SonarQube for IDE](https://www.sonarsource.com/products/sonarqube/ide/) — SonarQube for IDE (formerly SonarLint) is a free IDE extension available for IntelliJ, VS Code, Visual Studio, and Eclipse,  to find and fix coding issues in real-time, flagging issues as you code, just like a spell-checker.  More than a linter, it also delivers rich contextual guidance to help developers understand  why there is an issue, assess the risk, and educate them on how to fix it.
* **Code Intelligence** :warning: :copyright: — CI/CD-agnostic DevSecOps platform which combines industry-leading fuzzing engines for finding bugs and visualizing code coverage

Symbolic frontends cover Python, Java, JavaScript/TypeScript, C/C++ and C#. LLM findings are reported in a separate tier from symbolic ones, and the LLM layer runs on any OpenAI-compatible endpoint, including a local one.

#### [11. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Agent Orchestration & CLI Tools

* [MAO](https://trymao.dev/) - Native desktop canvas for macOS, Windows and Linux that runs Gemini CLI (0.41 or later) as a built-in agent alongside Claude Code, Codex, Grok, OpenCode, Antigravity and shell agents, each as a real terminal node on its own login. Wired agents hand each other tasks through the `mao` CLI, an Orchestrator agent can recruit and direct the others, and each layer is a git worktree whose diff you review and land from the canvas. Closed source; US$20 one-time, 7-day free trial.

##### Commands & Extensions

* [Recommerce (⭐10)](https://github.com/budna-marketplace/recommerce) ⭐ 10 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-28 - Agent Skills (SKILL.md) for second-hand purchases: compare listings, assemble kits, inspect items, plan collection, weigh keep vs. repair vs. replace, and recheck before committing. Read-only, no API key, never contacts sellers or buys. MIT. Install with `npx skills add budna-marketplace/recommerce -y -a gemini-cli`.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [Agent Notifications (⭐822)](https://github.com/777genius/agent-notifications) ⭐ 823 | 🐛 25 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-10-30 - Cross-platform desktop notifications and optional webhooks for Gemini CLI turn completion and tool permission requests. Also supports Claude Code, Codex CLI, OpenCode, Cursor CLI, and Qoder.
* [self-host (⭐5)](https://github.com/ajnart/self-host) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-10-28 - Lets your agent install self-hosted apps on your own hardware and set them up (admin accounts, API keys, apps connected to each other), asking before each step.
* [Scarwise (⭐0)](https://github.com/DomitorAI/scarwise) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-29 - Gemini CLI hooks (BeforeTool/AfterTool) that learn from failed tool calls by themselves and stop a known mistake before it runs; what one agent learns protects your other agents (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, OpenCode and more).

##### Education & Study Tools

* [codecall (⭐16)](https://github.com/GAURAV-1313/codecall) ⭐ 16 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-29 - Agent Skill that runs in Gemini CLI and other agents (`npx skills add GAURAV-1313/codecall`). Quizzes you on the code your agent just wrote, traces one request flow file by file, or plants one bug for a debugging-interview drill and restores it with Git. No API key.

##### MCP Servers

* [Coding Tools MCP (⭐435)](https://github.com/mybolide/coding-tools-mcp) ⭐ 435 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-10-28 - Desktop MCP workspace for coding agents: file edits, command execution, Git status and resumable history sessions behind one local stdio interface, so Gemini CLI gets project scaffolding and code search without four separate servers. Apache-2.0, official MCP Registry `io.github.mybolide/coding-tools-mcp`.
* [MCP Probe Kit (⭐41)](https://github.com/mybolide/mcp-probe-kit) ⭐ 41 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-30 - Diagnostics and self-test toolkit for MCP servers: enumerates every tool, resource and prompt, exercises them, and reports health, latency and schema drift between runs. Read-only against the target server, so you can point it at any server you connect Gemini CLI to and see what it actually exposes. MIT, `npx -y mcp-probe-kit`, official MCP Registry `io.github.mybolide/mcp-probe-kit`.
* [Budna MCP (⭐3)](https://github.com/budna-marketplace/mcp) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-10-30 - Read-only MCP server for Budna, a Swedish second-hand marketplace: search and inspect public listings, categories, filters, seller profiles, and bid and rating summaries from Gemini CLI. 11 tools, interactive listing cards in MCP Apps clients. Local stdio, no API key, no account. MIT. Run with `npx --yes @budna-marketplace/mcp`.
* [Tavero (⭐3)](https://github.com/baccaraaa/tavero) ⭐ 3 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-25 - MCP server and CLI that finds UI inconsistencies (spacing, colors, typography, component drift) in running React apps and gives coding agents fixes with file/line references. Works with Gemini CLI as an MCP server. Run: `npx -y tavero mcp`. Free, MIT.
* [Carbon Forge](https://carbonforge.dev/docs/p/guides/mcp-anonymous-access?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Hosted MCP server for source control and a shared record: Gemini CLI can read and write code, files and prose in one record on the server, open a lane instead of cloning, and land a change as one all-or-nothing changeset. Remote Streamable HTTP at `https://carbonforge.dev/api/mcp`; public workspaces read without a token, writing needs a bearer token.
* [Tera Studio](https://terastudio.co/ai-assistants/) - Hosted MCP server for singers over remote Streamable HTTP, no key or sign-in: `find_song_key` looks up a well-known song's key, tempo and typical vocal range by title, and the other tools return links to free browser tools that find a song file's key and BPM or move it up to 6 semitones. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http tera-studio https://terastudio.co/mcp`.
* [BankBridge](https://bankbridge.money/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Hosted MCP server that lets Gemini CLI answer questions about your own US bank, credit card and investment accounts: spending by category or merchant, recurring charges, monthly cashflow, holdings. 12 read-only tools, data fetched live on each call; it can't move money. Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth sign-in. Paid, $5/mo per connected bank. Install: `gemini extensions install https://github.com/bankbridge-money/bankbridge-plugin`, then `/mcp auth bankbridge`.

##### Graphics / Spritesheet Tools

* :tada: [Ludo Toolbox](https://ludo.ai/developers/toolbox) - Free, open-source (MIT) command-line tools that run offline: slice and join spritesheets, find a sprite's feet pivot and box/capsule colliders, check loops, recolor to Lospec palettes, cut tilesets, and export to Godot, Unity, Phaser and TexturePacker. Plain JSON output for scripts and coding agents.

#### [14. Awesome Amazon Seller](/content/ScaleLeap/awesome-amazon-seller/README.md)

##### Software and Tools

* [Inventory Optimizer](https://inventoryoptimizer.ai/) - Demand forecasting and restock planning for Amazon FBA, AWD and FBM, Shopify and QuickBooks. Formerly ForecastRx.
* [Perpetua](https://perpetua.io/) - Advertising software and managed services for Amazon, Instacart and other marketplaces, plus Amazon DSP and Google Ads. Formerly Sellics.
* [Threecolts Seller 365](https://www.threecolts.com/seller-365) - One subscription for ten Amazon seller tools: sourcing, listing, repricing, feedback requests and profit tracking. Includes the former FeedbackWhiz.

##### Podcasts

* [Keyword: The Amazon Insider Podcast](https://keywordpodcast.com/) - Hosted by Kate Valentine. Delivers authoritative content exclusively from former and current Amazon employees.

##### Blogs

* [ChannelX](https://channelx.world/) - News, articles and events for businesses that sell on online marketplaces. Formerly Tamebay.
* [Perpetua](https://perpetua.io/blog/) - Marketplace advertising strategy and Amazon PPC guides. Formerly the Sellics blog.

#### [20. Awesome Testing](/content/TheJambo/awesome-testing/README.md)

##### Software / API Testing

* [SolidPing (⭐26)](https://github.com/fclairamb/solidping) ⭐ 27 | 🐛 3 | 🌐 Go | 📅 2026-10-30 - Open-source (AGPL-3.0) uptime and API monitoring with a free hosted tier. Runs HTTP, gRPC, WebSocket and custom JS checks on a schedule and alerts when an endpoint fails.
* [Openhook (⭐1)](https://github.com/openhook-dev/openhook-inbox) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-29 - Open-source webhook inbox for capturing callbacks and inspecting request headers and bodies through a browser, REST API, or MCP.
* [API Spector](https://api-spector.dev) - Open-source API clien, mock server and contract testing tool. Zero telemetry.
* [BestJSON JSON Diff](https://bestjson.com/json-diff) - Free browser tool for comparing saved JSON API responses by structure, with explicit array-order and record-ID matching rules.
* [Kerno](https://kerno.io) - The AI Backend Runtime QA Engineer. Runs locally inside your coding agent's loop through MCP and tests each change against your running app and its real databases, queues and services.
* [OpenAPI Breaking Change Checker](https://martingruner.com/tools/openapi-breaking-change-checker) - Free browser tool that compares two Swagger 2.0 or OpenAPI 3.0/3.1 descriptions (JSON or YAML) and sorts each change into likely breaking, review-needed or compatible; the files stay in the browser.

##### Software / Security Testing

* [Darkmoon (⭐1.1k)](https://github.com/ASCIT31/Dark-Moon) ⭐ 1,168 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-27 - Open-source (GPLv3) autonomous AI penetration-testing CLI with 50 specialist agents and 140+ offensive tools over MCP for DAST-style web and API security assessment.

##### Software / AI & LLM Testing

* [OrcaReplay (⭐281)](https://github.com/Continuum-AI-Corp/OrcaReplay) ⭐ 287 | 🐛 2 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-01 - Records a coding-agent session below the harness, keeping the verbatim request and response bytes, then replays the same run offline with the network off or forks it from a checkpoint onto other models with a verify command deciding the verdict.
* [OrcaPromptVault (⭐80)](https://github.com/Continuum-AI-Corp/OrcaPromptVault) ⭐ 81 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-01 - Dated archive of the system prompts and tool-call schemas shipped AI agents send, so tests can be written against a product's real declared tool surface rather than a guess at it.
* [aiexpect (⭐2)](https://github.com/dmsehgal/aiexpect) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 4 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-09-14 - Python assertions for non-deterministic AI text that drop into pytest. Semantic and rule checks work offline; optional LLM judge with your own model; produces a Trust Score and a single-file HTML report.
* [flight-recorder (⭐1)](https://github.com/xag/flight-recorder) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-25 - Record every nondeterministic input your code reads (LLM answers, HTTP, database, clock, randomness) as one JSONL tape per request, then replay the tape against the real code offline, bit for bit, with the first divergence named. Open format with Python, Node, .NET, Go, Java and PHP implementations.
* [fanout-check (⭐1)](https://github.com/sunxiuguo/fanout-check) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-21 - Offline Python library and CLI that checks observed concurrency, overlap, and launch timing in explicitly grouped tool or agent calls.
* [geokit-cli (⭐0)](https://github.com/gstar-byte/geokit-cli) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-09-08 - Open-source CLI for automated AI search engine readiness testing, `/llms.txt` validation, and crawler access audits.

##### Software / Service Virtualization

* [MockForge (⭐12)](https://github.com/SaaSy-Solutions/mockforge) ⭐ 12 | 🐛 95 | 🌐 Rust | 📅 2026-10-30 - Open-source API mock server written in Rust for HTTP/OpenAPI, gRPC, GraphQL, WebSocket, Kafka, MQTT and AMQP, with data generation and latency/fault injection.
* [OutageFence (⭐0)](https://github.com/jn6825rsr7-afk/outagefence) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-21 - Local HTTP fault-injection CLI by Slanta Cause that checks synthetic sensitive data against declared destination rules, with positive controls and offline reports.
* [Twinbay](https://twinbay.ai) - Hosted, stateful twins of third-party APIs such as Shopify, Slack, Zendesk and QuickBooks for integration and AI agent tests. Each test run gets a private URL, and directives force states like rate limits or failed payments.
* [fieldtwin](https://nickgeorgeai.com/fieldtwin) - Open-source, local, stateful twins of the Jobber and QuickBooks Online APIs for integration and AI agent tests, with checkpoints, rewind, and faults on demand.

##### Software / Visual Testing

* [cloneproof (⭐0)](https://github.com/pcjansen09-max/cloneproof) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-29 - Scores how closely a website clone matches the original (pixels, layout, style, scroll motion) with Playwright and lists the exact differences.
* [CurlShot](https://curlshot.com) - Screenshot API for capturing baseline and comparison images of web pages in CI as PNG, JPEG, WebP or PDF, with full-page and CSS-selector element capture, device presets and ad/cookie-banner blocking. Free plan available.

##### Software / UI & End-to-End Testing

* [Browser Use (⭐119k)](https://github.com/browser-use/browser-use) ⭐ 119,538 | 🐛 627 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-30 - Open-source (MIT) Python AI agent that runs plain-English tasks in a real browser. Browser Use Cloud adds hosted stealth Chromium you can drive over CDP from Playwright, Puppeteer or Selenium, with session recordings and live view.
* [Device Hub Pro (⭐136)](https://github.com/gitemre/device-hub-pro) ⭐ 151 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-10-22 - Free, open-source macOS app for manual testing on Android emulators, Android phones and iOS simulators side by side, with live mirroring, device controls (network, location, permissions, deep links, push), logs, screenshots and screen recordings.
* [assay (⭐108)](https://github.com/awss1i/assay) ⭐ 109 | 🐛 1 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-29 - Deterministic command-line QA tool that serves a local web page, drives every control it finds in Chromium through Playwright. No tests to write and no LLM, with a Python API, an HTML report, and a plugin for AI coding agent. MIT, Python.
* [vibe-testing (⭐17)](https://github.com/AishwaryShrivastav/vibe-testing) ⭐ 17 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-15 - Open-source MCP server and CLI that scans web app routes and forms, runs browser checks with Playwright, and produces HTML QA reports.
* [App Crash Lab (⭐3)](https://github.com/aichance/business-ai-recipes/tree/main/tools/app-crash-lab) ⭐ 5 | 🐛 22 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-10-30 - MIT-licensed Node 22+/Playwright CLI that checks saved web-app values after reload and rejected updates, exporting state diffs and standalone Playwright tests.
* [ai-wright (⭐1)](https://github.com/TestChimp/ai-wright) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-14 - Open-source library that adds AI action, assertion, and data extraction steps to Playwright tests, using annotated screenshots and your own OpenAI, Gemini, or Claude API key.
* [Drengr Runner (⭐1)](https://github.com/SharminSirajudeen/drengr-runner) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Swift | 📅 2026-10-16 - Drive an iOS simulator over HTTP from any language. A 479-line Swift XCTest target replacing WebDriverAgent on simulators. Apache-2.0.
* [Proxy QA Browser (⭐1)](https://github.com/UbaidBinWaris/proxy_browser) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 12 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-10-29 - Free, open-source desktop app to test your own web forms by US location, emulated device and browser, with recorded scenarios, consent and accessibility checks, and a CI runner.
* [blastproof](https://blastproof.dev) - Open-source AI agent that runs plain-English e2e tests in a real browser. It reads the pull request diff, runs only the tests for the pages that changed and can block the merge on a score. Runs locally or in CI with your own LLM key.
* [cloudf.one](https://www.cloudf.one) - Rent a real Samsung Android phone in Singapore on a Singtel or M1 SIM and use it from a browser tab, to check how an app behaves for Singapore users. Manual use only, no Appium or scripts.
* [Clousd](https://clousd.com) - Cloud Android phones (150+ device models, Android 12-17) for app testing in a browser tab, over ADB, or through a REST API and MCP server for AI-driven tests.
* [ContextQA MCP](https://mcp.contextqa.com/docs) - Hosted MCP server for creating and running end-to-end tests and investigating failures, with OAuth browser sign-in.
* [Drengr](https://drengr.dev) - Mobile tests written as plain-English tasks instead of selectors, driven by an agent on real Android devices and iOS simulators. Works where there is no accessibility tree — canvas, games, Flutter without semantics labels — by falling back to vision and coordinates. Runs locally or in CI; bring your own model key.
* [VibeView](https://vibeview.io/) - Run & test iOS, Android, Apple TV and Android TV apps right in your browser, with Roku in beta. Build tests by recording a flow or write steps in plain English, flows can be ran cross-platform, and wired into pull requests. When a step fails - AI agent takes over so the rest of the run still completes, and the report tells you what changed based on failed steps and visual diff.
* [Device Changer](https://deviceschanger.org) - Applies device models, build fingerprints and identifiers to rooted Android test devices to reproduce device-specific bugs.

* [Checklist kiểm thử Web tiếng Việt (⭐0)](https://github.com/qakit-vn/checklist-kiem-thu-web) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-10-25 - Vietnamese-language web form and login testing checklist with copy-paste Vietnamese test data (diacritics, NFC/NFD, +84 phone numbers).
* [postal-code-formats (⭐0)](https://github.com/vinceblock99/postal-code-formats) ⭐ 0 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-09-16 - Postal code formats, validation regexes and 9,000 real sample codes for 31 countries, with every regex tested against the samples in CI.
* [8½ Minute Mail](https://8m30mail.com/) - Receive-only temporary inbox for manual email verification testing, with an 8-minute-30-second default lifetime and no account required.
* [TempMail Hub](https://tempmailhub.net/) - Free disposable inbox for manual or scripted signup and email-verification testing, with a selectable lifetime from 10 minutes to 7 days and no account required.
* [Mails.ai](https://mails.ai/docs/email-testing) - Email inboxes for automated tests: a plus address per test and one call that waits for the email and returns the sign-up code or magic link, with TypeScript and Python SDKs, Cypress and Playwright packages and an MCP server.
* [mailsocket](https://mailsocket.app) - Inbox API that waits for an email OTP or magic link and returns it as JSON, with Python and TypeScript SDKs and an MCP server.
* [ARADURU File Format Test Corpus](https://teamaraduru-hub.github.io/araduru-file-format-test-corpus/) - Open CC0 corpus of deterministic healthy and intentionally broken XLSX, DOCX, PPTX, ZIP, and PDF fixtures with SHA-256 manifests for file validation, parser, upload, and QA testing.
* [Código ao Ponto](https://codigoaoponto.com/en/tools) - Free browser-based test data generators and validators for Brazilian documents (CPF, CNPJ, RG, CNH) with valid check digits, plus test credit cards. No signup.
* [ByteRivet Sample Files](https://byterivet.com/test-files/) - Free browser-based generator for exact-size dummy files from 1 KB to 2 GB in 22 formats (PDF, PNG, JPG, DOCX, XLSX, CSV, JSON, ZIP, MP4, WAV and more) for upload-limit and file-handling tests. Files are built locally, no upload or signup.
* [StanzaAPI Test Data](https://stanzaapi.com/datasets) - Free CC0 synthetic test data for regulated B2B formats (IBAN, X12, ISO 20022, GS1, VAT, LEI, UDI, Peppol, Factur-X, CBAM, EPCIS), deterministic and check-digit-valid, as JSON and CSV.
* [FormFiller test data tools](https://formfillerapp.com/tools/) - Free browser-based generators for fake addresses, identities, IBANs, test card numbers and bulk CSV/JSON/SQL data in 22
  locales.

* [Spoof Me](https://chromewebstore.google.com/detail/spoof-me/onbjkmhileedchimkcmieeiomipdljki) - Test how a site behaves for users in other browsers, OSes, languages, timezones and screen sizes by switching the whole browser identity. Also available for Firefox.

Free instant scan; the full report is a one-off $29.
* [Barbwire](https://barbwire.ai) - Sends AI buyer personas built from your site through the live pages in a real browser, and reports where each one would stop buying, with a step-by-step replay, screenshots and fixes ranked worst first. 10 free runs, no card; paid plans from €49/month.
* [RateMyApp Testing Brief Builder](https://ratemyapp.io/tools/app-testing-brief-builder/) - Free, no-account tool for writing mobile usability-testing tasks, expected outcomes, and feedback instructions.
